- **patpat-debug**: Diagnose and fix a reproducible defect through root-cause evidence. - **patpat-engineer**: Execute one bounded implementation slice delegated by a named integration owner. Use only when ownership, forbidden scope, allowed mutations, and a proof contract are explicit; do not use for broad direct user requests. - **patpat-impact**: Trace and prove the blast radius of an existing or proposed repository change. - **patpat-inspect**: Explain repository behavior, ownership, placement, or historical design decisions without editing. Use for how, why, and diagnosis-only questions; not implementation or delivery. ([goiltpatpat/patpat](https://github.com/goiltpatpat/patpat/tree/HEAD/skills/patpat-inspect) · ★1)
- **patpat-learn**: Convert a recurring failure, correction, or verification gap from the active task into the smallest durable constraint. - **patpat-loop**: Run the explicit Patpat evidence-driven engineering loop. Use only when the user invokes /patpat, /patpat-loop, $patpat, or $patpat-loop, or directly asks to activate Patpat mode. ([goiltpatpat/patpat](https://github.com/goiltpatpat/patpat/tree/HEAD/skills/patpat-loop) · ★1)
- **patpat-perf**: Diagnose a resource regression or drive sustained numeric optimization against a measurable target. - **patpat-plan**: Design a falsifiable workflow for multi-phase repository work or a task with no safe narrow route. - **patpat-review**: Independently challenge an implementation, diff, or verification claim without editing. - **patpat-run**: Drive, checkpoint, validate, resume, or safely take over multi-phase engineering work through a deterministic local state machine. - **patpat-setup**: Inspect the active agent host and install or validate Patpat through its supported native plugin manager or an explicit Agent Skills directory. - **patpat-ship**: Commit and open or update a ready pull request after verified work when delivery intent exists, and merge only with explicit land or merge language. - **patpat-skill**: Create or revise a reusable agent skill with precise triggering, progressive disclosure, and behavioral proof. - **patpat-swarm**: Fan out isolated workers across slices or races and return one aggregated report. Use for /patpat-swarm, coverage matrices, parallel checks, or races with a declared selection rule. ([goiltpatpat/patpat](https://github.com/goiltpatpat/patpat/tree/HEAD/skills/patpat-swarm) · ★1)
- **patpat-verifier**: Create or maintain a project-specific verification skill that launches, exercises, observes, and cleans up the real system. - **patpat-verify**: Prove an implementation or repository claim against its authoritative artifact or user surface.
